英：An inverter chops DC into AC for a motor. Its DC-link capacitors are its reservoir, rated 600–800 V, and its own bleed resistors take minutes to bring them down to a safe level.

[codex:cx_fuelcell]
中：燃料电池
英：FUEL CELL STACK

[codex:cx_fuelcell_a]

英：Hundreds of bipolar plates between end plates and tie rods, with blower and humidifier.

[codex:cx_fuelcell_b]

英：With hydrogen and oxygen left, it generates: kill the bus and it's still live.

[codex:cx_fuelcell_c]

英：Switch it off at its own switch, shut and draw off the hydrogen, then remove it.

[codex:cx_fuelcell_x]
中：每片电池约 0.7 V，几百片串联到几百伏。电堆内的氢气即使阀门关了也会残留，泄漏到舱里就形成可燃云（氢的燃烧范围 4–75%，点火能极低）。
英：Each cell gives about 0.7 V; hundreds in series make hundreds of volts. Hydrogen stays in the stack after the valves shut, and leaked into a room it makes a flammable cloud (4–75 % in air, the smallest of sparks lights it).

[codex:cx_charger]

[codex:cx_charger_b]

英：With the cable plugged in, both ends may be live.

[codex:cx_charger_c]

英：A charger takes power from the bus and puts it into what it charges; if that has its own battery, the battery end of the lead is live too.

[codex:cx_dump_res]
中：泄能电阻
英：DUMP RESISTOR

[codex:cx_dump_res_a]
中：一排排来回折叠的不锈钢带，接在超导磁体的泄能回路上。
英：Banks of stainless ribbon folded back and forth, on a superconducting magnet's dump circuit.

[codex:cx_dump_res_b]
中：泄能时它把磁能变成热，满负荷时发红；拆了它磁体就无处泄能。
英：Dumping, it turns stored field energy into heat and glows; take it away and the magnet has nowhere to dump.

[codex:cx_dump_res_c]
中：磁体泄完能之前，别动它和它的母排。
英：Leave it and its busbars alone until the magnets are run down.

[codex:cx_dump_res_x]
中：线圈电流经泄能电阻衰减：I = I₀·e^(−t/τ)，τ = L/R。电阻越大泄得越快，但线圈两端电压 = I·R 也越高。几十吉焦的储能在几十秒内变成热，电阻带温度可升到几百度。
英：The coil's current falls through the resistor as I = I₀·e^(−t/τ), τ = L/R. More resistance dumps faster but puts more voltage (I·R) across the coil. Tens of gigajoules become heat in tens of seconds; the ribbon reaches hundreds of degrees.

[codex:cx_slip_ring]
中：滑环
英：SLIP RING

[codex:cx_slip_ring_a]

英：A stack of rings on the turning side and brushes on the fixed side: power into something spinning.

[codex:cx_slip_ring_b]

英：Brake a ring while its bus is live and the motor's regenerated current burns the slip ring.

[codex:cx_slip_ring_c]
中：先断环上的负载，再刹车消旋。
英：Take the ring's loads off before braking it.

[codex:cx_slip_ring_x]
中：电刷在铜环上滑动导电，电流密度有限；回馈电流和起弧会在环面烧出凹坑。它是旋转居住环唯一的电力通路。
英：The brushes slide on the rings to carry current, which has a limit; regenerated current and arcing pit the ring faces. It's the habitat ring's only way in for power.

[codex:cx_tray]

英：CABLE TRAY

[codex:cx_tray_a]

[codex:cx_tray_b]

英：One live cable in the bundle and none of it can be cut.

[codex:cx_tray_c]

[codex:cx_tray_x]

英：The tray is just metalwork; the danger is what it carries — power, signal and control mixed together. Check every circuit in it on the drawing before it comes out.

[codex:cx_module]

[codex:cx_pump]
中：离心泵组
英：CENTRIFUGAL PUMP SET

[codex:cx_pump_a]

[codex:cx_pump_x]

英：The impeller flings liquid to the volute's rim, turning speed into pressure. It hates running dry: with no liquid to cool and lubricate it, the mechanical seal burns within tens of seconds. Whole, a pump is worth far more than its metal.

[codex:cx_hx]

[codex:cx_hx_a]

英：A tube bundle in a shell: two fluids trading heat through the tube walls.

[codex:cx_hx_b]

英：Shell side and tube side are two fluids; one emptied, the other may still be full.

[codex:cx_hx_c]

英：Baffles send the shell-side fluid back and forth across the bundle to raise the heat transfer. Each side has its own pressure, temperature and medium, and its four nozzles are four separate jobs.

[codex:cx_radiator]
中：散热板
英：RADIATOR PANEL

[codex:cx_radiator_a]

英：Flow tubes between two headers across a fin sheet, radiating heat to space.

[codex:cx_radiator_b]
中：管里还有冷却液；系统还热时收拢或拆下，板会变形。
英：Its tubes still hold coolant; folded or removed while the system is hot, it deforms.

[codex:cx_radiator_c]

英：Let the loop carry the heat away and drain it before you take it.

[codex:cx_radiator_x]
中：太空中散热只能靠辐射：P = εσAT⁴。在 300 K，每平方米约排 400 W，所以散热板要做得很大。板面薄而轻，铰链接在吊杆上，抓住它要轻。
英：In space heat only leaves by radiation: P = εσAT⁴. At 300 K a square metre sheds about 400 W, which is why radiators are big. The panels are thin and light, hinged to their booms; handle them gently.

[codex:cx_chiller]
中：制冷机组
英：CHILLER

[codex:cx_chiller_a]
中：压缩机、冷凝器、膨胀阀和蒸发器组成的制冷回路。
英：Compressor, condenser, expansion valve and evaporator in one refrigeration loop.

[codex:cx_chiller_b]
中：一停或一切坏，它保冷的货舱或设备就开始回温。
英：Stopped or cut, whatever it keeps cold starts to warm.

[codex:cx_chiller_c]
中：先接上船坞供冷或处理完冷货，再停它。
英：Plug in berth cooling or deal with the cold cargo before stopping it.

[codex:cx_chiller_x]
中：制冷剂在蒸发器里吸热沸腾，被压缩后在冷凝器里放热凝结。回路里的制冷剂要从储液罐回收口闭环回收，不能放空。
英：The refrigerant boils in the evaporator taking heat in, is compressed, and condenses in the condenser giving it out. It's recovered in a closed loop from the receiver's port, never vented.

[codex:cx_freezer]
中：超低温冷柜
英：ULTRA-LOW FREEZER

[codex:cx_freezer_a]

英：A −80 °C freezer for samples or medicine: insulated door, controller, compressor bay.

[codex:cx_freezer_b]

英：Off, its contents slowly warm, and some samples are lost the moment they do.

[codex:cx_freezer_c]

英：Send it whole while powered, or check nothing in it needs the cold.

[codex:cx_freezer_x]
中：保温良好的冷柜断电后每小时回温几度；门一开就快得多。冷链货物的价值往往取决于它从没超过某个温度。
英：A well-insulated freezer warms a few degrees an hour unpowered, much faster with the door open. Cold-chain goods are often worth what they are only if they never went above a set temperature.

[codex:cx_env]
中：空气处理机
英：AIR HANDLING UNIT

[codex:cx_env_a]
中：进风阀、过滤器、冷却盘管、离心风机和送风箱。
英：Intake damper, filter, cooling coil, centrifugal fan and discharge plenum.

[codex:cx_env_b]
中：盘管里有冷却液，接水盘可能有积水；风机由总线供电。
英：Its coil holds coolant and its drain pan may hold water; the fan runs off the bus.

[codex:cx_env_c]
中：断电、排空盘管，再拆风管和底座螺栓。
英：Isolate, drain the coil, then the ducts and the base bolts.

[codex:cx_env_x]
中：空气处理机让舱里的空气循环、降温、除湿。它是舱室里最大的设备之一，沿气流方向一段段排开；舱里的空气压力和它无关，放气要走舱的接口。
英：It circulates, cools and dries a room's air. It's one of the biggest things in a room, laid out along its airflow; the room's pressure is nothing to do with it — vent through the room's own port.

[codex:cx_duct_fan]
中：风道轴流风机
英：AXIAL DUCT FAN

[codex:cx_duct_fan_a]
中：风道里的电机、叶片和导流叶。
英：A motor, blades and straightener vanes inside a duct.

[codex:cx_duct_fan_b]

英：Its terminal box arcs if opened live; the impeller may still be coasting.

[codex:cx_duct_fan_c]
中：断电，等叶轮停下，再拆法兰。
英：Isolate, let it spin down, then unbolt the flanges.

[codex:cx_duct_fan_x]
中：风机断电后叶轮靠惯性还能转一阵，真空里没有空气阻力，会转得更久。
英：An unpowered fan coasts on; in vacuum, with no air to slow it, for longer still.

[codex:cx_filter]
中：过滤器
英：CARTRIDGE FILTER

[codex:cx_filter_a]
中：壳体里的折叠滤芯，进出口、压差表和排放阀。
英：A pleated element in a housing, with inlet, outlet, a differential gauge and a drain valve.

[codex:cx_filter_b]
中：滤芯截留的东西可能有毒、有污染、或带放射性。
英：What the element caught may be toxic, contaminated or radioactive.

[codex:cx_filter_c]
中：先排空，带污染的滤芯封好送危险品口。
英：Drain it; a contaminated element is sealed and sent to the hazard mouth.

[codex:cx_filter_x]
中：压差表读滤芯两侧的压降，堵得越多压降越大。隔离舱、核设施、生物舱的过滤器本身就是危险品。
英：The differential gauge reads the drop across the element, rising as it clogs. In isolation rooms, reactors and bio bays the filter is itself a hazard.

[codex:cx_trap]
中：疏水器
英：CONDENSATE TRAP

[codex:cx_trap_a]
中：倒置浮桶式疏水器：排出冷凝水、留住蒸汽。
英：An inverted-bucket trap: lets condensate out, keeps steam in.

[codex:cx_trap_b]
中：蒸汽管路里的它可能又热又带压。
英：On a steam line it may be hot and under pressure.

[codex:cx_trap_c]
中：测温、泄压后再拆。
英：Measure the temperature, relieve the pressure, then remove it.

[codex:cx_trap_x]
中：浮桶里有蒸汽时浮起关阀，有冷凝水时下沉开阀。失重下它靠的是压差和流动，不是浮力——船上的疏水器都是改过的。
英：Steam floats the bucket and shuts the valve, water sinks it and opens it. Without gravity it works on pressure difference and flow rather than buoyancy — ship traps are modified for it.

[codex:cx_manifold]
中：阀组（总管）
英：VALVE MANIFOLD

[codex:cx_manifold_a]
中：一根总管上分出多路支管，每路一个隔离阀。
英：One header with several branches, each with its isolating valve.

[codex:cx_manifold_b]
中：每路支管里的介质、压力可能不同，拆总管时各路一起漏。
英：Each branch may hold a different medium at a different pressure; take the header off and they all leak.

[codex:cx_manifold_c]
中：逐路关阀、排空、测过，再拆总管。
英：Shut, drain and check branch by branch, then the header.

[codex:cx_manifold_x]
中：阀组让一台泵服务多个罐。开着的支路之间是连通的：抽一个罐时其它罐的阀没关，会一起被抽，或者在总管里混料。
英：A manifold lets one pump serve many tanks. Open branches are connected: draw one tank with the others' valves open and they're drawn too, or mixed in the header.

[codex:cx_aux]
中：调节阀
英：CONTROL VALVE

[codex:cx_aux_a]
中：球形阀体、阀盖、膜片执行器和定位器：自动调节流量的阀。
英：Globe body, bonnet, diaphragm actuator and positioner: a valve that sets a flow by itself.

[codex:cx_aux_b]
中：执行器断气断电时会自动走到“故障位”，可能开也可能关。
英：With its air or power gone it goes to its fail position, which may be open or shut.

[codex:cx_aux_c]
中：别靠它隔离；用手动阀隔离后再拆。
英：Don't rely on it to isolate; isolate with hand valves, then remove it.

[codex:cx_aux_x]
中：调节阀按控制信号连续开度，阀芯有间隙，本来就不是密封用的。故障开（FO）的阀在断电后全开。
英：A control valve throttles continuously to its signal; its trim has clearances and was never meant to seal. A fail-open valve goes fully open when the power goes.

[codex:cx_relief]
中：安全阀
英：PRESSURE RELIEF VALVE

[codex:cx_relief_a]
中：罐内压力过高时，安全阀会自动打开，把多余的气体排出去。
英：A spring holds the disc shut; over its set pressure it lifts and vents.

[codex:cx_relief_b]
中：安全阀如果冻住或堵塞，就无法泄压。罐内压力继续升高，可能发生爆罐。
英：Frozen or blocked, it won't lift: the pressure climbs until the vessel bursts.

[codex:cx_relief_c]
中：检查罐体温度和压力。阀门结冰就先解冻，也可以通过接口抽气泄压。
英：Read its body temperature; thaw a frozen one, or draw the pressure down at a port.

[codex:cx_relief_x]
中：安全阀是压力容器最后一道保护，整定压力通常是设计压力的 1.0–1.1 倍；起跳后压力降到回座压力才重新关闭。低温罐的安全阀出口会被泄放的冷气冻上冰塞。
英：The relief valve is a pressure vessel's last line, set at 1.0–1.1 × its design pressure; once lifted it reseats only below its reseat pressure. On a cryogenic tank, the cold gas it vents can freeze an ice plug in its own outlet.

[codex:cx_pipe]
中：管路
英：PIPE RUN

[codex:cx_pipe_a]
中：带法兰、保温和吊架的一段管道。
英：A length of pipe with flanges, lagging and hangers.

[codex:cx_pipe_b]
中：满管切开会漏液或喷出可燃物。
英：Cut full, it leaks or sprays something flammable.

[codex:cx_pipe_c]
中：上下游关阀、从低点排空，再拆法兰。
英：Valve it off both ends, drain from the low point, then unbolt the flanges.

[codex:cx_pipe_x]
中：拆法兰比切管干净：法兰螺栓松开后管段完整，可以整段回收。管段里残留的液体在失重下不会自己流出来，要靠抽。
英：Unbolting a flange is cleaner than cutting: the spool comes away whole. What's left inside won't run out by itself without gravity; it has to be drawn.

[codex:cx_hpu]
中：液压站
英：HYDRAULIC POWER UNIT

[codex:cx_hpu_a]
中：油箱、电机泵、蓄能器、电磁阀块和压力表。
英：Reservoir, motor-pump, accumulators, solenoid valve block, pressure gauge.

[codex:cx_hpu_b]
中：蓄能器里存着高压油：断电后压力还在，管一切就喷。
英：Its accumulators hold oil under pressure: off, the pressure stays, and a cut line sprays.

[codex:cx_hpu_c]
中：开泄压阀把蓄能器压力放到 0，再拆管和机构。
英：Open the dump valve, bring the accumulators to zero, then the lines and what they drive.

[codex:cx_hpu_x]
中：液压系统常在 20 MPa 以上工作：一个 20 L 的蓄能器在 20 MPa 时存约 200 kJ。它驱动的缸、夹钳、机械臂只要还保着压，就是一根压缩着的弹簧。
英：Hydraulics often run above 20 MPa: a 20 L accumulator at 20 MPa holds about 200 kJ. Every cylinder, clamp and arm it drives is a compressed spring while it holds pressure.

[codex:cx_actuator]
中：直线作动器
英：LINEAR ACTUATOR

[codex:cx_actuator_a]
中：电机、皮带、滚珠丝杠和推杆：把转动变成直线推力。
英：Motor, belt, ball screw and rod: rotation turned into a straight push.

[codex:cx_actuator_b]
中：带电时控制线一断，推杆可能猛地一动；它顶着的东西没锁会掉。
英：Live, a cut control line can jerk the rod; what it holds up drops if unlocked.

[codex:cx_actuator_c]
中：先锁住它或它支撑的东西，断电后再拆。
英：Lock it or what it carries, kill its supply, then remove it.

[codex:cx_actuator_x]
中：滚珠丝杠效率高，反过来也能被推动：断电后没有刹车的作动器会被负载推着倒转。精密场合（镜片促动器）有专门的锁定机构。
英：A ball screw is efficient enough to be back-driven: unpowered and unbraked, the load pushes it round. Precision uses — a mirror's actuators — have their own locks.

[codex:cx_gastank]
中：气瓶
英：GAS CYLINDER

[codex:cx_gastank_a]
中：带瓶阀和手轮的高压气瓶，色环标出气体种类。
英：A high-pressure cylinder with valve and handwheel, coloured bands naming the gas.

[codex:cx_gastank_b]
中：高压气瓶被切开才会爆裂：气体冲进舱里或漂散，瓶阀断掉时整瓶像火箭一样飞出去。
英：A full cylinder only bursts when cut: the gas floods the room or drifts off, and with its valve sheared it flies like a rocket.

[codex:cx_gastank_c]
中：密封完好的满瓶整瓶送设备回收口；要放气就从它的 -V 接口放，别切。
英：A sealed full cylinder goes whole to the equipment mouth; to empty it, vent at its -V port. Never cut it.

[codex:cx_gastank_x]
中：一只 50 L、200 bar 的气瓶储存约 1.5 MJ 的压缩能。瓶阀断掉时，喷口推力可达瓶重的几十倍。氧气瓶还会让周围的东西更容易燃烧。
英：A 50 L cylinder at 200 bar holds about 1.5 MJ. With its valve sheared off, the jet's thrust is tens of times its weight. An oxygen cylinder also makes everything round it burn more readily.

[codex:cx_fueltank]
中：推进剂 / 工艺储罐
英：PROPELLANT TANK

[codex:cx_fueltank_a]
中：卧在鞍座上的压力容器，内有防晃隔板，顶部管口和液位计。
英：A pressure vessel in its saddles, with baffles inside, nozzles on top and a level gauge.

[codex:cx_fueltank_b]
中：还有液或带压就切会爆；漏出的燃料成可燃云。
英：Cut with liquid or pressure in it, it bursts; leaked fuel makes a flammable cloud.

[codex:cx_fueltank_c]
中：从回收口抽空、泄掉罐顶气，整件送危险品口。
英：Draw it empty at its port, vent the ullage, take it whole to the hazard mouth.

[codex:cx_fueltank_x]
中：储罐上方的气垫（罐顶气）保持罐压，让泵吸得进液体。抽液时要么维持增压，要么让空气补进来；在真空里只抽不补，罐会被抽瘪。
英：The gas cushion above the liquid (the ullage) keeps the tank pressurised so the pump can draw. Drawing off, either keep the pressurisation on or let gas in; in vacuum, drawing alone can collapse the tank.

[codex:cx_sphere_tank]
中：球形压力罐
英：SPHERICAL TANK

[codex:cx_sphere_tank_a]
中：赤道环焊缝、安装耳、推进剂管理叶片和顶部加注阀。
英：A girth weld, mounting tabs, propellant management vanes and a fill valve on top.

[codex:cx_sphere_tank_b]
中：球罐承压最高：切开就是一次爆炸。
英：Spheres carry the highest pressures: cutting one is an explosion.

[codex:cx_sphere_tank_c]
中：测压，从接口泄压到安全，再拆安装耳。
英：Measure, relieve it at the port, then unbolt its tabs.

[codex:cx_sphere_tank_x]
中：同样壁厚，球的承压能力是圆筒的两倍，所以高压气体和推进剂常装在球罐里。失重下推进剂管理叶片靠毛细作用把液体引到出口。
英：For the same wall a sphere holds twice the pressure of a cylinder, so high-pressure gas and propellant go in spheres. In free fall the vanes wick the liquid to the outlet by capillary action.

[codex:cx_cryotank]
中：低温储罐
英：CRYOGENIC TANK

[codex:cx_cryotank_a]
中：真空夹套里的内罐：液氢、液氮或液化天然气在沸点下储存。
英：An inner tank in a vacuum jacket: liquid hydrogen, nitrogen or LNG stored at its boiling point.

[codex:cx_cryotank_b]
中：一直在蒸发升压；安全阀冻住就会爆罐。
英：It boils off all the time and its pressure climbs; with its relief valve frozen it bursts.

[codex:cx_cryotank_c]
中：保持制冷，边抽边降压，最后再断电。
英：Keep it refrigerated, draw it down to lower the pressure, cut the power last.

[codex:cx_cryotank_x]
中：液氢在 −253 °C、液氮在 −196 °C 沸腾。热量每漏进一点，就蒸发一点液体变成气体，体积膨胀几百倍——这就是低温罐压力不断上升的原因。真空夹套一破，漏热增加十倍。
英：Liquid hydrogen boils at −253 °C, nitrogen at −196 °C. Every bit of heat that leaks in boils some liquid off, expanding hundreds of times — which is why a cryogenic tank's pressure keeps rising. Lose the vacuum jacket and the heat leak goes up tenfold.

[codex:cx_water]
中：水箱
英：WATER TANK

[codex:cx_water_a]
中：带人孔、通气管、防晃板和液位镜的水箱。
英：A tank with manway, vent, baffles and a sight glass.

[codex:cx_water_b]
中：切开后水漂出来，碰到带电设备就短路。
英：Cut open, the water drifts out and shorts what it touches.

[codex:cx_water_c]
中：从出水阀或接口抽空，再拆。
英：Draw it off at the outlet or a port, then remove it.

[codex:cx_water_x]
中：水在真空里先剧烈沸腾，蒸发吸热让剩下的水在几秒内结冰，成为缓慢升华的冰球。水是船上值钱的货：几立方米的水按合同价计。
英：In vacuum water boils hard at first; the evaporation chills the rest into a slowly subliming ball of ice within seconds. Water is worth money: cubic metres of it are paid into the contract.

[codex:cx_extinguisher]
中：灭火瓶
英：FIRE SUPPRESSION BOTTLE

[codex:cx_extinguisher_a]
中：带释放头、压力表和电磁启动器的灭火剂瓶。
英：A suppressant bottle with discharge head, gauge and solenoid actuator.

[codex:cx_extinguisher_b]
中：启动器带电时误触发会喷放；瓶本身是高压容器。
英：Its actuator, if live, can fire it; the bottle itself is a pressure vessel.

[codex:cx_extinguisher_c]
中：需要灭火时用它；不用就断电后整瓶送危险品口。
英：Use it if there's a fire; otherwise isolate it and send it whole to the hazard mouth.

[codex:cx_extinguisher_x]
中：船上常用 CO₂、哈龙替代品或泡沫。在密闭舱里喷放 CO₂ 能把氧浓度降到燃烧下限以下，但也意味着这个舱里的气体不能再按空气回收。有的船上灭火瓶早已空了——测一下压力表。
英：Ships carry CO₂, halon replacements or foam. Discharged in a closed room CO₂ takes the oxygen below what burns, though the room's gas is no longer clean air to recover. Some ships' bottles are long empty — read the gauge.

[codex:cx_o2cab]

英：EMERGENCY OXYGEN CABINET

[codex:cx_o2cab_a]

英：Two oxygen cylinders behind a glass door, a regulator and a mask.

[codex:cx_o2cab_b]

英：High-pressure pure oxygen: a laser near it makes any fire far fiercer.

[codex:cx_o2cab_c]

英：Shut the cylinder valves and send the cabinet whole to the hazard mouth.

[codex:cx_o2cab_x]

英：Oxygen doesn't burn itself but makes almost anything burn faster and hotter; oxygen-soaked fabric, grease, even metal swarf can light.

[codex:cx_co2_scrubber]

英：CO₂ SCRUBBER

[codex:cx_co2_scrubber_a]

英：The beds take turns: one absorbs the cabin's CO₂ while the other desorbs to vacuum. Above 1 % CO₂ people get headaches; above 5 % it's dangerous.

[codex:cx_psa]
中：变压吸附分离器
英：PRESSURE-SWING ADSORPTION

[codex:cx_psa_a]

英：Two adsorber columns and switching manifolds: one gas separated out of a mixture.

[codex:cx_psa_b]
中：塔里有压力，分离出的可能是纯氧、纯氮或氢。
英：The columns are under pressure, and what they separate may be pure oxygen, nitrogen or hydrogen.

[codex:cx_psa_c]
中：关进气，从接口泄压，再拆。
英：Shut the feed, relieve it at a port, then remove it.

[codex:cx_psa_x]
中：分子筛在高压下多吸附某种分子、低压下放出，两塔交替加压减压，就能连续产出纯气。纯氮环境会让人无预兆地窒息。
英：A molecular sieve takes up one kind of molecule at high pressure and lets it go at low; two columns swinging in turn make a steady stream of pure gas. Pure nitrogen suffocates without warning.

[codex:cx_electrolyser]
中：电解制氧机
英：WATER ELECTROLYSER

[codex:cx_electrolyser_a]
中：电解槽、氢氧气液分离器、循环泵和整流器。
英：Cell stack, hydrogen and oxygen separators, circulation pump and rectifier.

[codex:cx_electrolyser_b]
中：同时有氢、有氧、有电：泄漏就是爆鸣气。
英：Hydrogen, oxygen and power in one place: a leak is a detonating mixture.

[codex:cx_electrolyser_c]

英：Isolate it, vent each gas separately, then remove it.

[codex:cx_electrolyser_x]
中：电解 1 kg 水产生约 1.24 m³ 氢气和 0.62 m³ 氧气，耗电约 5 kWh。氢氧以 2:1 混合是最猛烈的爆鸣气。
英：Splitting 1 kg of water gives about 1.24 m³ of hydrogen and 0.62 m³ of oxygen for some 5 kWh. Mixed two to one they make the most violent of detonating gases.

[codex:cx_humidifier]

英：STEAM HUMIDIFIER

[codex:cx_humidifier_a]

英：Electrode boiling cylinder, fill and drain valves, steam hose, control box.

[codex:cx_humidifier_b]

英：Hot water and live electrodes in the cylinder.

[codex:cx_humidifier_c]

英：Isolate, drain, then remove it.

[codex:cx_humidifier_x]

英：An electrode humidifier boils water by passing current straight through it; the water's conductivity sets the power. The water stays hot after it's switched off.

[codex:cx_coriolis]

[codex:cx_coriolis_b]

[codex:cx_coriolis_x]

英：Fluid in a vibrating tube feels a Coriolis force; the two ends fall out of phase in proportion to the mass flow. Good to 0.1 %, and expensive.

[codex:cx_reel]

[codex:cx_flywheel_rotor]

英：Brake cooling on, then the brake; under 300 rpm, remove it.

[codex:cx_flywheel_rotor_x]
中：飞轮储能 E = ½Iω²，与转速的平方成正比：20,000 rpm 时储能是 2,000 rpm 的一百倍。真空和磁悬浮让它几乎没有摩擦，自己能转好几天。
英：A flywheel holds E = ½Iω², the square of its speed: at 20,000 rpm a hundred times what it holds at 2,000. Vacuum and magnetic bearings leave it almost no friction; on its own it spins for days.

[codex:cx_reaction_wheel]

[codex:cx_despin_brake]
中：消旋制动器
英：DESPIN BRAKE

[codex:cx_despin_brake_a]

英：The disc, calipers and cooling fins on a habitat ring's hub.

[codex:cx_despin_brake_b]
中：环上负载带电时刹车，回馈电流会烧滑环；干刹会烧刹车。
英：Brake with the ring's loads live and the regenerated current burns the slip ring; brake dry and the brake burns.

[codex:cx_despin_brake_c]
中：断环上负载、开冷却，两个环一起刹。
英：Loads off the ring, cooling on, brake both rings together.

[codex:cx_despin_brake_x]
中：电机-制动器把环的动能转成电存进电容（再生制动），电容满了要放电才能继续刹。只刹一个环，船体会被另一个环的角动量拧扭。
英：The motor-brake turns the ring's energy into charge in a capacitor (regenerative braking); full, it has to be dumped before braking goes on. Brake only one ring and the other's angular momentum twists the hull.

[codex:cx_rotary_seal]

[codex:cx_rotary_seal_a]

[codex:cx_rotary_seal_b]

英：Open the transfer airlock while the ring turns and it tears.

[codex:cx_rotary_seal_c]

英：Only once the ring has stopped and its bearing is locked.

[codex:cx_rotary_seal_x]
中：迷宫密封靠一圈圈齿的节流减少泄漏，加缓冲气槽隔开两侧，本身不接触——所以它能在转动中密封，但挡不住真正的开口。
英：A labyrinth seal throttles leakage through ring after ring of teeth with a buffer-gas groove between, without touching — sealing while it turns, but no use against a real opening.

[codex:cx_spoke_lift]

英：Nb₃Sn has no resistance below about 18 K and is kept at 4.5 K in liquid helium. In a quench a patch turns resistive, current times resistance makes heat, the heat takes more conductor normal — and in seconds the coil's whole store becomes heat and force.

[codex:cx_m_munitions]

[codex:cx_m_munitions_x]

英：The beam working near a round keeps heating it until it reaches its cook-off temperature; hitting something fast enough sets it off too. The magazine's heat test point reads its hottest round. Wetted rounds fear neither heat nor knocks — and are worth nothing.

[codex:cx_m_quench]

英：SUPERCONDUCTING MAGNET QUENCH

[codex:cx_m_quench_a]

英：Eight coil packs holding field energy, kept superconducting by liquid helium and the cold box.

[codex:cx_m_quench_b]
中：失冷超过临界温度、或带场切支撑，线圈失超：能量瞬间释放，并连锁到相邻线圈。
英：Lose the cooling past the critical temperature, or cut a support while energised, and it quenches — all at once, and the coils beside it follow.

[codex:cx_m_quench_c]
中：合上泄能开关、保持冷箱有电，储能读数降到安全值再断冷却、拆线圈。
英：Close the dump switch, keep the cold box powered, wait for the stored energy to fall to safe, only then the cooling and the coils.

[codex:cx_m_quench_x]
中：泄能时电流经泄能电阻按 e^(−t/τ) 衰减，几个时间常数后降到安全值以下，这时线圈升温也无害。顺序反过来（先断冷却），线圈在满能量时升温越过临界温度，失超释放的热和电磁力会毁掉线圈和周围设备，还会烧掉一部分昂贵的液氦。
英：Dumping, the current decays through the resistor as e^(−t/τ) and after a few time constants is below safe; then the coil can warm harmlessly. The other way round — cooling off first — it warms past critical while full, and the quench's heat and forces wreck it and what's near, boiling off costly helium.

[codex:cx_m_flywheel]
中：还在转的飞轮
英：A FLYWHEEL STILL SPINNING

[codex:cx_m_flywheel_a]
中：飞轮开局 20,000 rpm；刹车、冷却和转速表管着它。
英：The flywheel comes in at 20,000 rpm; brake, brake cooling and a tachometer go with it.

[codex:cx_m_flywheel_b]
中：不开冷却干刹，8 秒后卡钳烧坏，从此停不下来；300 rpm 以上松开，轮子甩出去。
英：Brake it dry and the caliper burns out in 8 s and it can't be stopped; free it above 300 rpm and it's thrown.

[codex:cx_m_flywheel_c]
中：先开刹车冷却再上刹车，等到 300 rpm 以下再拆；轨道预紧从尾往头松。
英：Brake cooling on, then the brake; under 300 rpm, take it out. Slack the rail preloads from the tail forward.

[codex:cx_m_flywheel_x]
中：开着冷却时刹车每秒降 250 rpm，从 20,000 降下来约 80 秒；干刹降速减半。飞轮能量与转速平方成正比，300 rpm 时只剩开局的 0.02%。两组电容断主电后仍带 400 V，喂着上轨的直线电机：先放电。
英：With cooling on the brake takes 250 rpm off a second, about 80 s down from 20,000; dry, half as fast. The energy goes with the square of the speed: at 300 rpm, 0.02 % of what it started with. Two capacitor banks keep 400 V on the upper rail's linear motor after the mains is off: discharge them first.

[codex:cx_m_pyro]
中：救生舱火工品
英：ESCAPE POD PYROTECHNICS

[codex:cx_m_pyro_a]
中：每个救生舱两个发射夹，前夹装着爆炸螺栓，接着它自己的发射电容。
英：Each pod has two launch clamps; the front one carries an explosive bolt fed by its own launch capacitor.

[codex:cx_m_pyro_b]
中：待发电路还带电时拆发射夹或火工品，螺栓走火，救生舱被弹出去。
英：Free the clamp or the charge with the arming circuit live and the bolt fires: the pod is thrown out.

[codex:cx_m_pyro_c]
中：在竖井的救生舱控制面板断开待发开关，再拆夹子；火工品送危险品口。
英：Disarm it at the pod control panel in the shaft, then the clamps; the charges go to the hazard mouth.

[codex:cx_m_pyro_x]
中：发射电容是一路独立电源，主开关管不到它。爆炸螺栓本身是危险品，不能进熔炉。某些合同里会有一个救生舱的发射电路仍然带电（“救生舱待发”事故）。
英：The launch capacitor is a supply of its own, beyond the main switch. The bolts are hazards and never go to the furnace. Some contracts have one pod whose circuit is still armed.

[codex:cx_m_magnet]
中：残磁
英：RESIDUAL MAGNETISM

[codex:cx_m_magnet_a]
中：磁力牵引头断电后还留着磁场。
英：The magnetic towing head keeps a field after its power is off.

[codex:cx_m_magnet_b]
中：范围内会把无人机和散落的钢件吸过去撞上。
英：It pulls the drone and loose steel in range onto itself.

[codex:cx_m_magnet_c]
中：在线圈电源断开后，用退磁控件消磁，再靠近。
英：With its coil supply dead, degauss it at its control before going near.

[codex:cx_m_magnet_x]
中：铁磁材料磁化后会保留剩磁；退磁是用逐渐衰减的交变磁场把磁畴打乱。退磁要求线圈本身没电，否则会重新磁化。
英：Ferromagnetic material keeps some magnetisation; degaussing scrambles its domains with a decaying alternating field — which needs the coil's own supply dead, or it simply re-magnetises.

[codex:cx_m_preload]
中：预紧机构
英：PRELOADED MECHANISMS

[codex:cx_m_preload_a]
中：靠液压蓄能器或锁销撑着、储着力的臂、轴、铰链和缆。
英：Arms, shafts, hinges and cables held under load by accumulators or locking pins.

[codex:cx_m_preload_b]
中：锁销还在、或液压还有压就拆枢轴，它会弹开，砸坏它和它撞上的东西。
英：Take the pivot out with a pin still in or pressure still on, and it springs, smashing itself and what it hits.

[codex:cx_m_preload_c]
中：先泄蓄能器的压，再拔全部锁销，张力表归零，最后拆枢轴。
英：Bleed the accumulator, pull every pin, see the tension gauge reach zero, then the pivot.

[codex:cx_m_preload_x]
中：预紧储存的是弹性能和液压能：拉缆 E = F²L/2EA，蓄能器 E ≈ pV·ln(p/p₀)。卸能的顺序总是“能量—锁—结构”。拆完锁销但没泄压，力全落在枢轴上，枢轴一松就释放。
英：Preload stores elastic and hydraulic energy: a cable E = F²L/2EA, an accumulator roughly pV·ln(p/p₀). The order is always energy, then locks, then structure. Pins out but pressure still on, all the force sits on the pivot, released the moment it goes.

[codex:cx_m_drones]
中：各自带电的无人机
英：DRONES WITH THEIR OWN POWER

[codex:cx_m_drones_a]
中：机库里每个机位、每架无人机都有自己的电池。
英：Every bay and every drone in the hangar has a battery of its own.

[codex:cx_m_drones_b]
中：断了主电池它们照样带电；电池还在机身上就松开，无人机醒来把自己甩进机位。
英：Kill the main battery and they're still live; release one with its pack aboard and it wakes and throws itself into its bay.

[codex:cx_m_drones_c]
中：断机位充电开关、断机身电池开关，拔两根线，摘电池包，再整机运走。
英：Charger off at the rack, battery off on the airframe, both leads out, pack off — then the drone, whole.

[codex:cx_m_drones_x]
中：无人机的飞控在失去链路时会尝试自主起飞；只有电池物理断开才算安全。整机交付设备口价值远高于熔成废料。
英：A drone's controller tries to take off on its own when its link drops; only a physically disconnected battery is safe. Delivered whole it's worth far more than melted.

[codex:cx_m_cryo_tank]
中：低温货罐
英：CRYOGENIC CARGO TANKS

[codex:cx_m_cryo_tank_a]
中：真空夹套里的低温货罐，压力跟着温度走。
英：Cold cargo tanks in vacuum jackets, their pressure following their temperature.

[codex:cx_m_cryo_tank_b]
中：回温升压；安全阀冻住时压力冲到爆罐。夹套破了漏热快十倍。
英：Warming raises the pressure; with the relief frozen it climbs to burst. A broken jacket leaks heat ten times faster.

[codex:cx_m_cryo_tank_c]
中：保持制冷机运行，从货罐接口抽液；冻住的阀先加热解冻。
英：Keep the chillers running, draw the cargo at each tank's port, thaw a frozen valve with its heater.

[codex:cx_m_cryo_tank_x]
中：有液体时罐的蒸气压随温度升高大约每 20 °C 翻一倍。真空夹套完好时漏热很慢，一单合同里升不到 10 °C；夹套一破就快得多。
英：With liquid in it the vapour pressure roughly doubles every 20 °C. A sound vacuum jacket lets heat in slowly — under 10 °C in a contract's time; broken, far faster.

[codex:cx_m_boiloff]
中：蒸发升压
英：BOIL-OFF

[codex:cx_m_boiloff_a]
中：低温罐一直在蒸发，罐压持续上升；制冷运转时压力保持。
英：The tanks boil off and their pressure keeps climbing; with refrigeration running it holds.

[codex:cx_m_boiloff_b]
中：先断电，制冷一停，压力爬到安全阀放空（货物损失）或爆罐。
英：Cut the power first and the cooling stops: the pressure climbs to the relief (cargo lost) or to burst.

[codex:cx_m_boiloff_c]
中：边制冷边从软管抽液，抽得越多压力越低；最后才断电。
英：Refrigerate while you draw the cargo off at the hose — the more drawn, the lower the pressure — and cut the power last.

[codex:cx_m_boiloff_x]
中：蒸发的气体占液体几百倍的体积，所以一点点漏热就让压力明显上升；保温层破损处升得更快。安全阀起跳放掉的就是货物本身。
英：Boil-off gas takes hundreds of times the liquid's volume, so a little heat leak raises the pressure fast, faster where the insulation is torn. What the relief vents is the cargo itself.

[codex:cx_m_frozen_valve]
中：冻住的安全阀
英：A FROZEN RELIEF VALVE

[codex:cx_m_frozen_valve_a]
中：安全阀出口结了冰，阀门被冻住，无法正常泄压。
英：One tank's relief valve has an ice plug in its outlet and won't lift.

[codex:cx_m_frozen_valve_b]
中：罐内压力超过安全值，阀门也打不开。压力继续升高，可能导致爆罐。
英：Past its set pressure it doesn't relieve; the tank climbs on until it bursts.

[codex:cx_m_frozen_valve_c]
中：先测阀门温度。如果结冰，就加热解冻；也可以先抽走罐内气体，降低压力。
英：Read each relief valve's temperature: thaw the abnormally cold one with its heater, or draw its tank down first.

[codex:cx_m_frozen_valve_x]
中：泄放的低温气体在出口膨胀降温，空气或水汽在出口冻成冰塞。测温探针能发现它：冻住的阀比其它阀冷得多。
英：Cold gas venting through the outlet expands and chills it, freezing moisture into an ice plug. The probe shows it: the frozen valve reads far colder than the rest.

[codex:cx_m_dilution]
中：稀释制冷机按序回温
英：DILUTION FRIDGE WARM-UP

[codex:cx_m_dilution_a]
中：量子芯片放在混合室里，温度接近绝对零度。外面有好几层冷板，靠制冷机一直降温。
英：The qubit chip sits in the mixing chamber at millikelvin, hung under stage after stage.

[codex:cx_m_dilution_b]
中：混合气还没回收就停冷头，气体受热沸腾，可能损坏冷板。芯片温度太低时直接加热，也可能裂开。
英：Stop the cold head before recovering the mixture and it boils off; heat it or touch it while still at millikelvin and the chip cracks.

[codex:cx_m_dilution_c]
中：先停循环泵，回收混合气，再停脉管冷头。等冷板自然回温后再加热，拆卸前锁住隔振台。
英：Stop circulation → recover the mixture → stop the pulse tube → let the stages warm, heaters only past the coldest → lock the isolation table before any cutting.

[codex:cx_m_dilution_x]
中：混合室最冷、回温最慢。在低于热震温度时打开加热器，芯片表面和内部温差太大，硅片会裂；低于脆性温度时任何振动都能让它碎。隔振台没锁，附近的切割会通过台面震到芯片。
英：The mixing chamber is coldest and warms slowest. Heaters switched on below the shock temperature put too much difference between the chip's surface and its body and the silicon cracks; below the brittle temperature any shake breaks it. With the isolation table unlocked, a nearby cut shakes the chip.

[codex:cx_m_coldchain]
中：冷链不能断
英：THE COLD CHAIN

[codex:cx_m_coldchain_a]
中：三个冷舱 −25 °C，靠冷机和制冷主机保温。
英：Three cold holds at −25 °C, held there by the chillers and the refrigeration plant.

[codex:cx_m_coldchain_b]
中：断电或切坏制冷，冷舱回温；过了 −5 °C 货物解冻，只剩 45% 价值。
英：Cut the power or the plant and the holds warm; past −5 °C the cargo thaws and keeps 45 % of its value.

[codex:cx_m_coldchain_c]
中：先在外部面板插上“船坞临时供冷”，再断电、再动制冷系统。
英：Plug in berth cooling at the outside panel first, then cut the power and work the plant.

[codex:cx_m_coldchain_x]
中：冷冻货物的价值来自“从未解冻”：一次回温就不可逆。制冷剂从储液罐回收口闭环回收；开舱顶货舱口前先放气。
英：Frozen cargo is worth what it is because it never thawed: one warm-up can't be undone. The refrigerant is recovered at the receiver's port; vent each hold before opening its roof hatch.

[codex:cx_m_ice]
中：冰体
英：THE ICE

[codex:cx_m_ice_a]
中：整座冰山就是货：每块冰 0.6 m³ 水，靠加热垫逐块融化。
英：The iceberg is the cargo: each block 0.6 m³ of water, melted block by block with the heater pads.

[codex:cx_m_ice_b]
中：融水积到 3 m³ 不抽、总线带电，发动机舱短路；鞍座夹着时切裂纹处的冰，冰体裂开。
英：Let 3 m³ of melt pile up with the bus live and the engine room shorts; cut the cracked blocks with the saddles still clamped and the ice splits.

[codex:cx_m_ice_c]
中：开加热垫，边融边从水总管接口抽走；冰剩一半以下再松鞍座。
英：Heaters on, draw the melt off at the water header as it comes; release the saddles only once less than half the ice is left.

[codex:cx_m_ice_x]
中：融化 1 kg 冰要 334 kJ。冰切得很慢——切口会重新冻上。应变表读冰体裂纹的扩展程度（开局 90%）。
英：Melting a kilogram of ice takes 334 kJ. Ice cuts very slowly, its kerf freezing over again. The strain gauge reads how far the crack has run (90 % at the start).

[codex:cx_m_drill_torque]
中：钻杆残余扭矩与泥浆压力
英：TRAPPED TORQUE AND MUD PRESSURE

[codex:cx_m_drill_torque_a]
中：八节立根还扭着残余扭矩，管里憋着泥浆压力。
英：Eight stands still wound with residual torque, mud pressure trapped inside.

[codex:cx_m_drill_torque_b]
中：带扭矩卸扣，钻杆反弹扭断、甩打钻台；带压卸扣，泥浆喷出污染设备。
英：Break a stand with torque on and it whips round, smashing the drill floor; with pressure on, the mud blows out over the equipment.

[codex:cx_m_drill_torque_c]
中：关转盘刹车泄扭（扭矩表 < 5%），开泄压阀放泥浆（可回收），再卸扣。
英：Release the rotary brake to bleed the torque (gauge under 5 %), open the relief and recover the mud, then break out.

[codex:cx_m_drill_torque_x]
中：扭矩泄放按时间常数指数下降；泥浆从泄压阀放到回收口是有价值的货。扭矩表和压力表都在钻台上。
英：The torque bleeds off exponentially; the mud drained through the relief is cargo worth recovering. Both gauges are on the drill floor.

[codex:cx_m_drill_slips]
中：卡瓦与自上而下
英：SLIPS, TOP DOWN

[codex:cx_m_drill_slips_a]
中：每节立根下面一副卡瓦，托着它和它下面的整串钻杆。
英：Under each stand a set of slips, holding it and everything below it.

[codex:cx_m_drill_slips_b]
中：先卸下面的，上面的失去支撑掉落；卡瓦没坐好就卸，下面的整串掉进井。
英：Break a lower stand first and those above lose their support and drop; break out with the slips open and the string below drops down the hole.

[codex:cx_m_drill_slips_c]
中：从最上面一节开始，确认下面的卡瓦坐着，逐节往下卸。
英：Start with the top stand, check the slips under it are set, and work down.

[codex:cx_m_drill_slips_x]
中：卡瓦是楔形牙块，钻杆越重咬得越紧。钻台作业的铁律：任何时候钻杆都要被卡瓦或吊卡之一抓着。
英：Slips are wedged dies that grip harder the heavier the pipe. The rig floor's rule: at every moment the string is held by either the slips or the elevator.

[codex:cx_m_tanknet]
中：交叉连通罐
英：CROSS-CONNECTED TANKS

[codex:cx_m_tanknet_a]
中：三个罐经交叉阀接到一根总管，大泵从泵房回收口抽总管。
英：Three tanks on one header through crossover valves; the big pump draws the header at the pump room's port.

[codex:cx_m_tanknet_b]
中：先断电泵就抽不动；抽干还开着泵干转损坏；先泄罐顶气再抽，罐被吸瘪。
英：Cut the power first and the pump can't draw; leave it on dry and it wrecks itself; vent the ullage before drawing and the tank collapses.

[codex:cx_m_tanknet_c]
中：开交叉阀，带着增压抽完，关回收口，再关增压、泄罐顶气；支管残液从低点排放口抽。
英：Crossovers open, draw it off pressurised, close the port, then pressurisation off and ullage vented; drain the branches at their low points.

[codex:cx_m_tanknet_x]
中：开着交叉阀的罐液位会经总管拉平（连通器）。增压阀开着时，氮气球罐保持罐压，抽液不会抽出真空。有的合同里一个交叉阀显示关、实际内漏：关着时那个罐液位还在降。
英：Tanks with open crossovers level out through the header. With the pressurisation open, the nitrogen sphere keeps the ullage pressure up and drawing can't pull a vacuum. In some contracts a crossover reads shut but leaks: that tank's level keeps falling with it closed.

[codex:cx_m_immersion]
中：浸没冷却
英：IMMERSION COOLING

[codex:cx_m_immersion_a]
中：十二个机柜各泡在一槽绝缘冷却液里。
英：Twelve racks, each standing in a tank of dielectric coolant.

[codex:cx_m_immersion_b]
中：槽里有液就吊机柜，液体泼洒，短路同一排相邻的机柜。
英：Lift a rack with its tank full and the fluid spills, shorting the racks beside it in the row.

[codex:cx_m_immersion_c]
中：每个槽从接口抽干，再吊机柜。
英：Draw each tank dry at its port before lifting its rack.

[codex:cx_m_immersion_x]
中：冷却液本身很贵，按体积计价；失重下泼出的液团会漂到相邻机柜上。
英：The coolant is dear and paid by volume; in free fall what spills drifts onto the next racks.

[codex:cx_m_custody]
中：数据监管
英：DATA CUSTODY

[codex:cx_m_custody_a]
中：存储阵列的硬盘是客户的监管物，要锁进屏蔽运输箱。
英：The storage drives are the client's property in custody, to be latched into shielded transport bays.

[codex:cx_m_custody_b]
中：监管锁扣没拨就拔出硬盘，算违约罚款。
英：Pull a drive with its custody latch open and it's a breach, fined.

[codex:cx_m_custody_c]
中：先拨每块盘的监管锁扣，运输箱整件送设备口。
英：Throw every drive's latch; take the transport bays whole to the equipment bay.

[codex:cx_m_custody_x]
中：运输箱本身是合同指定件。锁扣拨上后，硬盘是客户封存的财产，完整计价。
英：The transport bays are named items. Latched, the drives are the client's sealed property and pay whole.

[codex:cx_m_waterball]
中：失重水球
英：THE WATER BALL

[codex:cx_m_waterball_a]
中：网罩里团着几十立方米的水，里面养着鱼。
英：Tens of cubic metres of water held in a net, fish living in it.

[codex:cx_m_waterball_b]
中：网罩或支架被切、被拆，水球散开：水损失，舱里电气泡水，总线带电就短路，鱼死。
英：Cut or free the net or its frame and it bursts: the water lost, the room's electrics soaked (shorted if the bus is live), the fish dead.

[codex:cx_m_waterball_c]
中：先用泵把水抽到安全量以下（水球会变小），再拆网罩；鱼苗箱是指定件。
英：Pump it down below the safe fraction first (the ball shrinks as you go), then the net; the fish holding tank is a named item.

[codex:cx_m_waterball_x]
中：没有重力，表面张力把水团成球；网罩只是轻轻兜着它。30 m³ 水是 30 吨，散开后的水团以原有的速度四处漂。
英：With no gravity surface tension rounds the water into a ball; the net only cradles it. 30 m³ is 30 tonnes, and burst, it drifts apart at whatever speed it had.

[codex:cx_m_media]
中：外观相同的三种介质
英：THREE LOOK-ALIKE MEDIA

[codex:cx_m_media_a]
中：溶剂、酸液和乙二醇三只罐，三路管子一样且在途中交叉。
英：Solvent, acid and glycol in three tanks; their three identical pipes cross on the way to the header.

[codex:cx_m_media_b]
中：抽一路时另两路阀没关，三种混成废液，一文不值还要付处理费。
英：Draw one with the others' valves open and they mix into waste: worthless, and you pay for its disposal.

[codex:cx_m_media_c]

[codex:cx_m_media_x]

英：Header port 1 is tank 3, port 2 is tank 1, port 3 is tank 2 — guess and you're wrong. Take the header off with liquid in the branches and they all leak, fined by volume. The separator keeps some pressure; relieve it at its port.

[codex:cx_m_o2_flash]

英：OXYGEN-RICH FLASH FIRE

英：Rooms whose oxygen is well above air's 21 %, each different.

[codex:cx_m_o2_flash_b]

英：With pressure in it and oxygen over the threshold, a laser nearby flashes the whole room: equipment burnt, the drone too.

[codex:cx_m_o2_flash_c]

英：Read each room's oxygen, vent each through its own port, then use the laser.

[codex:cx_m_o2_flash_x]
中：氧浓度从 21% 升到 35% 以上，可燃物的点燃温度大幅下降、燃速成倍提高，织物和塑料几乎一点就着。舱门打开，两舱空气会混合。
英：From 21 % to over 35 % oxygen, ignition temperatures fall and burning rates multiply; fabric and plastics light at almost nothing. Open a door between two rooms and their air mixes.

[codex:cx_m_composite]
中：复合材料毒烟
英：COMPOSITE FUMES

[codex:cx_m_composite_a]

英：The airframe panels are carbon-fibre composite, bolted on.

[codex:cx_m_composite_b]

[codex:cx_m_composite_x]

英：The epoxy matrix pyrolyses under the beam into carbon monoxide, hydrogen cyanide and fine fibre dust. Venting the room takes the smoke out with the air.

[codex:cx_m_radiation]
中：辐射阴影
英：RADIATION SHADOW

[codex:cx_m_radiation_a]

[codex:cx_m_radiation_b]

[codex:cx_m_radiation_c]

[codex:cx_m_radiation_x]

英：Dose rate falls with the square of distance: at 7 m about 65 mSv/h, and a minute or so there is trouble (each second counts two minutes' dose). The shadow is wherever the shield is — on its mounts or on the crane. The dose probe reads the rate where it sits.

[codex:cx_m_decay_heat]
中：停机不等于冷却
英：STOPPED ISN'T COLD

[codex:cx_m_decay_heat_a]
中：炉芯或堆芯停了还有几百度，只靠冷却回路降温。
英：A furnace or reactor core stopped is still hundreds of degrees, cooled only by its loop.

[codex:cx_m_decay_heat_b]
中：高温时断了冷却，热冲击把它震裂；没冷透就吊出、收散热瓣，同样开裂变形。
英：Cut the cooling while it's hot and thermal shock cracks it; lift it or fold its radiator petals before it's cold and it cracks or deforms the same.

[codex:cx_m_decay_heat_c]
中：保持泵有电、回路满，读温度等它冷透，再断电、再吊出。
英：Keep the pump powered and the loop full, watch the temperature down, then cut power and lift it.

[codex:cx_m_decay_heat_x]
中：冷却回路工作时约每秒降 4 °C。MF-126 的炉芯 350 °C 以上断冷却会裂，150 °C 以上吊出会裂；NR-95 的堆芯 60 °C 以上收散热瓣会变形。
英：With the loop running it comes down about 4 °C a second. MF-126's core cracks if the cooling goes above 350 °C and if lifted above 150 °C; NR-95's petals deform if folded above 60 °C.

[codex:cx_m_thermal]

英：PERIHELION HEAT

[codex:cx_m_thermal_b]

[codex:cx_m_thermal_c]

[codex:cx_m_thermal_x]

英：In vacuum each zone sheds heat only by radiation, at its own rate; the zones the cooling loop reaches cool much faster.

[codex:cx_m_bio]

英：NEGATIVE PRESSURE AND BIOHAZARD

[codex:cx_m_bio_a]

[codex:cx_m_bio_b]

英：Seal every biohazard item, vent through the recovery hose's filter, then open doors.

[codex:cx_m_bio_x]

英：Negative pressure keeps the air flowing into the isolation room so nothing gets out; lose it (a door open, a wall cut) and the flow reverses. The recovery hose's filter takes contamination out of the air.

[codex:cx_m_balance]
中：按受力顺序释放
英：RELEASE IN ORDER OF LOAD

[codex:cx_m_balance_a]
中：卷扬机的缆还绷着；两台外挂吊舱一上一下全挂在尾后。
英：The winch's cable still taut; two outboard pods hang aft, one above, one below.

[codex:cx_m_balance_b]
中：刹车没松就拆卷扬轴，卷筒甩出；只拆一台吊舱，船体被另一台拽扭。
英：Take the winch shaft with the brake on and the drum flies; take one pod alone and the other twists the hull.

[codex:cx_m_balance_c]

英：Pull the brake pins (tension to zero) first; set the hull support at the stern, or take both pods together.

[codex:cx_m_balance_x]
中：对称挂着的两个重物互相平衡；去掉一个，剩下那个的力矩全由船体承受，超出就扭坏吊梁和支柱。锁喉夹块受力来自卷扬机的缆，要刹车、锁销、蓄能器三样都处理掉。
英：Two weights hung symmetrically balance each other; take one and the hull carries the other's whole moment, twisting its beams and struts. The throat clamp's load comes from the winch cable: brake, pins and accumulator all have to be dealt with.

[codex:cx_m_sandwich]
中：拆三明治
英：UNMAKING THE SANDWICH

[codex:cx_m_sandwich_a]
中：上下两条船体用 8 个液压夹钳夹着中间的居住舱。
英：Two hulls clamp the habitat between them with eight hydraulic clamps.

[codex:cx_m_sandwich_b]
中：货舱没放气就松夹钳会弹开；一侧比另一侧多松两个，货舱扭转、裂了的横梁 2 断掉。
英：Release a clamp before the hold is vented and it springs open; get two ahead on one side and the hold twists and cracked beam 2 snaps.

[codex:cx_m_sandwich_x]
中：横梁 2 已经开裂，它的载荷表读 192 kN（其余 120 kN）。夹钳还保着压就整件拆下，钳口弹开。三套电路各自独立：上体走主电池，下体和货舱各有自己的电池。
英：Beam 2 is already cracked; its load cell reads 192 kN (the others 120 kN). A clamp taken off while still holding pressure springs its jaw. Three separate circuits: the upper hull on the main battery, the lower hull and the hold each on their own.

[codex:cx_m_boxbeam]
中：货柜就是承力梁
英：THE CONTAINERS ARE THE BEAM

[codex:cx_m_boxbeam_a]
中：货柜四角扭锁锁在一起，整列货柜就是船体的承力箱梁。
英：Containers twist-locked corner to corner: each column of them is the hull's load-bearing box beam.

[codex:cx_m_boxbeam_b]
中：外侧还满着就卸空里面一列，那里折弯；同列从下面抽，上面压下来；拉杆还紧着切会抽回来。
英：Empty an inner column while the outer ones are full and it kinks there; pull from the bottom of a column and the ones above come down; cut a tie rod still tight and it whips.

[codex:cx_m_boxbeam_c]
中：从两端往中间卸，每列从上往下；先松花篮螺丝（拉力计读 0）再拆拉杆；有压的箱先抽气。
英：Unload from the ends inward, each column top down; slack the turnbuckles (gauge at zero) before the tie rods; pump down pressurised boxes first.

[codex:cx_m_boxbeam_x]
中：船坞夹着第 4 列；每一列的弯曲 = 外侧还在位货柜的量 × 力臂，除以本列的刚度（弦杆 + 在位货柜数）。应变片读本列弯曲占极限的百分比。住人箱 60 kPa、低温箱 450 kPa。
英：The berth holds column 4. Each column's bending is the containers still outboard of it times their lever, over its own stiffness (chords plus containers in place). Strain gauges read it as a share of the limit. Accommodation boxes hold 60 kPa, cryo boxes 450 kPa.

[codex:cx_m_sail]
中：帆的张力网
英：THE SAIL'S TENSION NET

[codex:cx_m_sail_a]
中：4 台绞盘的刹车各绷着一个象限的薄膜和撑索（每根 12 kN）。
英：Four winch brakes each hold a quadrant's film and stays taut (12 kN a stay).

[codex:cx_m_sail_b]
中：绷着切薄膜就撕，裂口跑到两边；切撑索，角梁甩向吊舱；没接地就碰薄膜，静电打无人机。
英：Cut the film taut and it tears, the tear running on; cut a stay and the corner spar whips at the gondola; touch the film unearthed and static jumps to the drone.

[codex:cx_m_sail_c]
中：接地 GND → 右侧检修门洞进通道 → 舱门旁 G-G 抽气 → 开舱门 HT-G → 松刹车 W → 开收帆电机 F：四角卷成帆卷。
英：GND earth, G-G vent, HT-G hatch, W brakes off, F motors on: four rolls.

[codex:cx_m_sail_x]
中：整帆收起来的做法：左下角接地线 GND；外框和撑索把吊舱四面围住，只有右侧外框留了检修门洞，一条没有帆膜的检修通道直通舱门，从这里飞进去才不会撞破帆膜；舱门旁的气口 G-G 接软管抽到 5 kPa 以下（舱里有气，舱门开不得）；开舱门 HT-G 进去；松开四台绞盘刹车 W-NW / W-NE / W-SE / W-SW，四个角松下来；再开四台收帆电机 F-NW / F-NE / F-SE / F-SW，每个角把薄膜一块块卷上绞盘；卷好的四个帆卷拆下送设备口。刹车没松就开电机会过载撕膜，没接地会被静电打出针孔；切薄膜只剩废料。张力计读每个象限的拉力。右上角那块开局就撕了，挨着它切也会撕穿。帆面面积大、电阻高，太阳风让它积累了大量静电。
英：To furl it whole: earth it bottom-left (GND); the frame and stays close the gondola in on every side: the right edge spar has a service gate, and a film-free lane runs from it to the hatch, the one way in without holing the film; vent the gondola below 5 kPa at G-G beside the hatch (it won't open with air in it); open the hatch HT-G and go in; let off the four winch brakes W-NW/NE/SE/SW so the corners go slack; switch on the four furling motors F-NW/NE/SE/SW to wind each corner's film onto its winch; unbolt the four rolls for the equipment mouth. A motor on before its brake is off overloads and tears the film; unearthed, static pinholes it; cut film is only scrap. The tension gauges read each quadrant's pull. The top-right panel is already torn, and cutting next to it tears through. A sail is a huge, highly resistive surface; the solar wind has charged it heavily.

[codex:cx_m_softshell]
中：软壳气囊
英：SOFT-SHELL GASBAGS

[codex:cx_m_softshell_a]

英：Each gasbag is a 30 kPa volume whose shape is only its pressure; each has a tensioner and straps.

[codex:cx_m_tether]

英：A SPINNING TETHER

[codex:cx_m_tether_a]

[codex:cx_m_tether_b]

[codex:cx_m_tether_c]

[codex:cx_m_tether_x]

英：Tension is 40 kN × (rpm/2)² plus the tensioner's 8 kN. With cooling, the brake takes 0.025 rpm off a second. The worn length has 5 of 7 strands: cut it stopped but still tensioned and the whole tether parts.

[codex:cx_m_rails]

英：RAIL-LOCKED CARGO

[codex:cx_m_rails_a]
中：货物和滑轨之间是轨道锁，不是螺栓：扳手卸不掉。
英：Cargo sits on its rail behind rail locks, not bolts: no wrench frees it.

[codex:cx_m_rails_b]
中：门没开就解锁，货撞门；前面一件还锁着就放后面的，后一件撞前一件；切托着货的轨道，货被扯坏。
英：Unlock with the doors shut and it hits them; release one behind a locked one and it rams it; cut the rail carrying it and it's torn.

[codex:cx_m_rails_c]
中：先放气、开货门（门靠总线供电，断电前开），再从最前面一件开始解锁，让货沿轨道开出。
英：Vent, open the cargo doors (on bus power — before you cut it), then unlock from the front, letting each piece run out along its rail.

[codex:cx_m_rails_x]
中：货物出轨道末端后制动到 0.4 m/s 慢慢滑出；前一件没运走就放下一件会撞上。SV-122 的仪器挂在减振吊架上：还有仪器锁着就切主梁或减振连杆，整架砸下。
英：Off the rail's end the cargo is braked to 0.4 m/s and drifts out; release the next before the last is moved and they collide. SV-122's instruments hang in a damped frame: cut its beam or links with instruments still locked to it and the whole frame drops.

[codex:cx_m_rings]
中：还在转的居住环
英：HABITAT RINGS STILL TURNING

[codex:cx_m_rings_a]
中：两个居住环约 3 rpm 旋转，靠轮毂的电机-制动器消旋。
英：Two habitat rings turning at about 3 rpm, despun by the motor-brakes at their hubs.

[codex:cx_m_rings_b]
中：环上负载带电时刹车会烧滑环；只刹一个环船体被拧；环还转就锁轴承，轴承卡死。
英：Brake with the ring's loads live and the slip ring burns; brake only one and the hull twists; lock the bearing while it turns and it seizes.

[codex:cx_m_rings_c]
中：断环上负载 → 开制动冷却 → 两环一起刹（电容满了放电）→ 停稳后锁轴承 → 再开过渡气闸。
英：Ring loads off → brake cooling on → brake both together (dump the capacitor when full) → lock the bearing once still → only then the transfer airlocks.

[codex:cx_m_rings_x]
中：制动把环的动能回馈进电容，电容满了刹车就不起作用，要放电。两环转速差太大持续太久，船坞夹具过载。环上的门对着压力低得多的一侧打开，冲击会打碎玻璃。
英：Braking feeds the ring's energy into a capacitor; full, the brake stops working until it's dumped. Two rings too far apart in speed for too long overload the berth's clamps. A ring room's door opened onto far lower pressure breaks its glass.

[codex:cx_m_spine]
中：主轴受力
英：THE SPINE'S LOAD PATH

[codex:cx_m_spine_a]
中：尾部的反应堆、发动机和推进剂挂在 440 m 主轴上，弦杆是传力路径。
英：The tail — reactor, engines, propellant — hangs on the 440 m spine; its chords carry the load.

[codex:cx_m_spine_b]
中：切一根弦杆，剩下的弦杆分担整个尾部；超出能力那一段撕裂，尾部下坠扭转。
英：Cut a chord and the rest carry the whole tail; past what they take, that segment tears and the tail sags and twists.

[codex:cx_m_spine_c]
中：先把尾部的重量拆下来，弦杆最后切。
英：Take the weight off the tail first; cut the chords last.

[codex:cx_m_spine_x]
中：每一段承受它后面还在船上的所有重量，由还完整的弦杆平分。应变探头读各段受力。
英：Each segment carries everything still aboard aft of it, shared by its chords that are still whole. Strain probes read each segment's load.

[codex:cx_m_workday]
中：多日作业
英：A JOB OF SEVERAL DAYS

[codex:cx_m_workday_a]
中：这艘船太大，一个工作日拆不完。
英：This ship is too big for one working day.

[codex:cx_m_workday_b]
中：每多一天，合同就多一天的运营成本。
英：Every extra day adds a day's running cost to the contract.

[codex:cx_m_workday_c]
中：规划好顺序，减少无用功；收工时一切自动保存，第二天接着干。
英：Plan the order to waste nothing; at the end of a day everything is saved and work goes on next day.

[codex:cx_m_workday_x]
中：工作日按合同的游戏时间计算。收工时整个物理状态保存，下一天在原处继续。
英：Days are counted from the contract's play time. At day's end the whole physical state is saved and the next day picks up where it stood.

[codex:cx_m_mirror_dust]
中：镜面污染
英：MIRROR CONTAMINATION

[codex:cx_m_mirror_dust_a]
中：主镜镜片极贵、易碎，有一张卷帘式防尘罩。
英：The primary's segments are very dear and fragile; there is a roll-down dust cover.

[codex:cx_m_mirror_dust_b]
中：镜面附近的切割把金属粉尘撒在镜面上，每次切割都掉价值。
英：Every cut near an uncovered segment throws metal dust on its face and takes value off.

[codex:cx_m_mirror_dust_c]
中：先放下防尘罩（电机要有电），再在附近切割。
英：Run the dust cover down (its motor needs power) before cutting anywhere near.

[codex:cx_m_mirror_dust_x]
中：防尘罩是电动卷帘，没电不能放下；跟着卷轴一起拆走就没了。粉尘污染到一定程度后就不再更糟。
英：The cover is a motorised blind and won't run without power; taken away with its roller, it's gone. Dust alone can only take a segment down so far.

[codex:cx_m_mirror_lock]
中：促动器锁与外圈先拆
英：ACTUATOR LOCKS, OUTER RING FIRST

[codex:cx_m_mirror_lock_a]

英：Each ring of segments has an actuator lock switch, which only latches while the mirror control cabinet has power.

[codex:cx_m_mirror_lock_b]
中：促动器没锁就拆镜片，镜片掉出镜座碎裂；外侧还有镜片时拆内侧，外侧的边缘崩掉。
英：Free a segment with its ring unlocked and it drops out and cracks; free an inner one with an outer one still on and the outer's edge chips.

[codex:cx_m_mirror_lock_c]
中：断电前锁好所有圈的促动器，从外圈到内圈拆。
英：Lock every ring before the power goes, and take them from the outside in.

[codex:cx_m_mirror_lock_x]
中：外圈镜片的内缘搭在内圈镜片上，所以必须由外向内。
英：An outer segment's inner edge is carried by the one inside it, so it has to go outside-in.

[codex:cx_m_glass]
中：承压玻璃与烤裂
英：GLASS UNDER PRESSURE AND HEAT

[codex:cx_m_glass_a]
中：观景玻璃、穹顶玻璃和精密罩板，用压条螺栓固定，背后扛着舱压。
英：Viewing glass, dome glass and precision panels, held by clamping strips with the room's pressure behind them.

[codex:cx_m_glass_b]
中：舱里还有压就拆，玻璃被推出去碎掉；附近 0.7 m 内动激光，玻璃烤裂。
英：Unbolt it with pressure inside and it's blown out and shatters; work the laser within 0.7 m and it crazes.

[codex:cx_m_glass_c]

英：Vent first, cut well away, unbolt the strips and lift it out whole.

[codex:cx_m_glass_x]
中：一块 2 m² 的观景玻璃在 100 kPa 下受 20 吨推力，压条螺栓一松就失去约束。PS-12 的中央竖井两层的门同时开着时，两层成了一个舱，放一边会把另一层一起带走。
英：A 2 m² viewing pane at 100 kPa carries twenty tonnes; loosen its strips and nothing holds it. On PS-12, with both of the shaft's doors open on two decks, those decks are one room, and venting one takes the other with it.

[codex:cx_m_vault]
中：双路电锁库门
英：THE TWO-SUPPLY VAULT LOCK

[codex:cx_m_vault_a]
中：库门电磁铁走两路电：主总线，和检修柜里的门锁备用电源。
英：Each vault door's solenoid has two supplies: the main bus and a backup in the service locker.

[codex:cx_m_vault_b]
中：断了外部主开关，测点读零，门锁和总线照样带电——备用电源经电磁铁倒灌回总线。
英：Kill the main switch outside and the test point reads zero, but the lock and the bus are still live — the backup feeds back through the solenoid.

[codex:cx_m_vault_c]
中：进舰尾检修柜断备用电源，两路都断后扳释放杆，库放气，再开门。
英：Go into the stern service locker and kill the backup; with both dead, pull the release lever, vent the vault, open it.

[codex:cx_m_vault_x]
中：门锁带电时库门打不开，释放杆也扳不动；扳了以后门自己就能开，不需要电。保管箱整件运走。
英：While the lock has power the door won't open and the lever won't move; once pulled, the door opens on its own, no power needed. The deposit boxes go out whole.

[codex:cx_m_nested]
中：船中船
英：A SHIP INSIDE A SHIP

[codex:cx_m_nested_a]
中：货舱里装着一艘完整的小飞船，合同要它整艘完好取出。
英：A whole small ship rides in the hold; the contract wants it out whole.

[codex:cx_m_nested_b]
中：小船任何一块外壳被切或拆，就不再“完整”，只按零件算；托架锁先松船首侧，它会撞向舱门。
英：Cut or take any of its hull and it's no longer whole, only parts; release a bow-side cradle lock first and it lurches into the door.

[codex:cx_m_nested_c]
中：先处理小船自己的电池、燃料和舱压，从尾到首松托架锁，开首门，开绞车整体拖出。
英：Make it safe first — its battery, fuel and air — release the cradle locks stern first, open the bow door, run the winch to slide it out.

[codex:cx_m_nested_x]
中：整艘完好的小船按一个整体计价，远高于它的零件（溢价约是零件价值的 7 倍）。撞上舱门会扣掉一部分溢价、碰坏它的设备。
英：Whole, the small ship is priced as one, far above its parts (about seven times their value). Lurching into the door costs part of that premium and knocks its fittings.

[codex:cx_s_ullage]
中：带压罐与气垫
英：PRESSURISED TANKS AND THE ULLAGE

[codex:cx_s_ullage_a]
中：推进剂罐顶部是一层高压气垫（增压气），把液体压向出口。
英：Above a propellant tank's liquid sits a cushion of high-pressure gas that drives it to the outlet.

[codex:cx_s_ullage_b]
中：带压时切开，气垫会把推进剂一起喷出来；整罐切断，几秒内排空。泄压后的罐只会慢慢渗。
英：Cut it under pressure and the cushion blows the propellant out with it; sever the tank and it empties in seconds. A vented tank only seeps.

[codex:cx_s_ullage_c]
中：先关增压，再从排气口放掉气垫，最后才抽液、拆罐。
英：Shut the pressurisation off first, vent the ullage at its port, only then draw it down and take it out.

[codex:cx_s_ullage_x]
中：增压气不断补进来，罐压就降不下去，放气只是白放；所以顺序是先断气源、再放气垫。气垫放掉后，罐里只剩液体自己的蒸气压，破口处只是渗出，不再喷射。
英：While the pressurant keeps coming the tank pressure won't fall and venting is wasted, so the order is: cut the supply, then vent the ullage. With it gone only the liquid's own vapour pressure is left, and a hole seeps instead of jetting.

[codex:cx_m_hypergolic]
中：自燃推进剂
英：HYPERGOLIC PROPELLANTS

[codex:cx_m_hypergolic_a]
中：甲基肼罐（T-1、T-3）和四氧化二氮罐（T-2）分开装，T-2 不接燃料总管。
英：Methylhydrazine in T-1 and T-3, nitrogen tetroxide in T-2; T-2 is not on the fuel manifold.

[codex:cx_m_hypergolic_b]
中：两种一碰就着，不需要火花，真空里也会爆；爆燃按 TNT 当量计伤害半径。
英：The two light the moment they meet, no spark needed, even in vacuum; the blast's reach goes by its TNT equivalent.

[codex:cx_m_hypergolic_c]
中：T-2 从它自己的排放口 LD-2 单独抽；两种的管线和罐别同时破口。
英：Draw T-2 alone at its own drain LD-2; never have both kinds' lines or tanks open at once.

[codex:cx_m_hypergolic_x]
中：自燃推进剂省掉了点火器，发动机开关几百次都可靠，所以常用于姿控和变轨。代价是两种都剧毒、腐蚀，泄漏的蒸气碰上就起火。拆解时它们必须在不同的时间、从不同的口子出来。
英：Hypergolics need no igniter, so an engine relights reliably hundreds of times: that is why they fly in thrusters and orbit-raising stages. The price: both are toxic and corrosive, and their vapours ignite on contact. Taking a ship apart, they leave at different times through different ports.

[codex:cx_s_contents]
中：先测再切：容器里有什么
英：MEASURE BEFORE YOU CUT: WHAT'S INSIDE

[codex:cx_s_contents_a]
中：灭火瓶、气瓶、储罐大多不是空的：测量工具扫过去会多一行“内容物”读数。
英：Extinguishers, cylinders and tanks are mostly not empty: the measuring tool shows a CONTENTS line for each.

[codex:cx_s_contents_b]

英：A gas port is named <tag>-V, a liquid port <tag>-D; a tank with liquid in it is heavier, too heavy to grab.

[codex:cx_s_contents_c]
中：先测，看清是什么、多少压力，再决定：放、抽，还是整件送走。
英：Measure first: what it is, at what pressure. Then decide: vent, draw, or send it whole.

[codex:cx_s_contents_x]
中：灭火瓶里是约 5.7 MPa 的二氧化碳，氧气瓶是 15 MPa 的氧气，还有氮气、压缩空气、蓄能器、氦气和氢取样瓶；液罐里是水、泥浆、泡沫液、润滑油或服务推进剂。同样的外形，切开后的后果完全不同。
英：Extinguishers hold CO2 at about 5.7 MPa, oxygen cylinders 15 MPa of oxygen; there are nitrogen, compressed air, accumulators, helium and hydrogen sample bottles; tanks hold water, mud, foam, lube oil or service propellant. The same shape, cut open, ends very differently.

[codex:cx_s_o2_rich]
中：富氧助燃
英：OXYGEN-RICH AIR

[codex:cx_s_o2_rich_a]
中：舱里氧气超过正常空气的 21%：氧气瓶破了，或者舱本来就富氧。
英：A room's air with more oxygen than the usual 21 %: a cylinder burst, or the room was rich to begin with.

[codex:cx_s_o2_rich_b]
中：氧越多，越容易点着、烧得越猛；切口、火花一碰就着。氢气本身也能烧。
英：The more oxygen, the easier anything lights and the fiercer it burns: a cut or a spark is enough. Hydrogen burns on its own.

[codex:cx_s_o2_rich_c]

英：Measure the room's oxygen; over the mark, vent the room before the laser.

[codex:cx_s_o2_rich_x]
中：空气里的氮气稀释了氧，也吸走燃烧放出的热。氧浓度一旦超过 30%，布料、塑料、润滑油的燃速成倍上升；纯氧环境里连钢丝都能被点燃。
英：The nitrogen in air thins the oxygen and soaks up a fire's heat. Above 30 % oxygen, fabric, plastics and oil burn several times faster; in pure oxygen even steel wool will burn.

============================================================
# 十五、短标签（四个字以内的按钮、栏目名、单位）（1686 条）
# 同样的字在很多界面出现时只列一次，「# 在」写了出现在哪些界面、共几处；改这里一条，所有这些地方一起改。
# 这些字要短：按钮和栏目的宽度是按现在的字数排的，多一两个字可能放不下（合并后会跑超框检查）。
============================================================

[short:1]
# 在：暂停菜单、设置页（2 处）
中：设置
英：SETTINGS

[short:2]
# 在：暂停菜单（1 处）
中：继续
英：RESUME

[short:3]
# 在：暂停菜单（1 处）
中：再次确认
英：CLICK AGAIN TO CONFIRM

[short:4]
# 在：设置页（1 处）
